# Catalog of cited works

[Home](../README.md) · [catalog.json](../data/catalog.json) · [BibTeX](../data/references.bib)

All 565 works cited in the survey and its supplementary material, newest first. `source-derived` metadata comes from the manuscript bibliography; `primary-identity-checked` records were compared with a primary source, which checks bibliographic identity only.
